Finance defines the capital needed by economic activities, while financing defines the tools for supply of capital. The financial system, with the help of financial tools, is the process that collects and deals with those who are more than needed by individuals and institutions. Financing systems are a means of providing the capital needed by their economic activities. The financial system that arises in classical banking transactions is to use these funds for a certain interest. The non-interested financial system is to provide the needed capital without interest. One of them is the institutions that are created around the world based on savings, the idea of financing. The idea of savings-based financing has been popularized worldwide since the 19th century since the first public institution with construction savings polls established in England in 1775. The main idea of building savings chests is that people who want to own a dwelling or a vehicle can save money by paying a fixed account, and when they reach a certain level of savings, the right to loan is born. They have a similar structure with banks, but they also have models that pay interest to participants, as well as non-bank-stabilized models that have interest-free models. This system has been implemented in our country as it has been known for more than 30 years as a public union system. In particular, this savings system, which is preferred by citizens who approach the concept of interest, is common at the point of housing and transportation. Instead, it would be more appropriate to speed up the process by giving a 48-hour period of decision on access to the judiciary. Matter needs to be edited in this direction. Article 2 of the law proposal requires that the paid capital be 100m Turkish liras. Only one out of 34 official companies still operating in the field of austerity financing can meet that requirement. There are dozens of companies that don't have $100 million in capital. There are millions of citizens who don't get loans from banks, who go to these companies to own a property. By this law, you're going to create new victims when you say we're going to organize companies. Congressmans, this law proposal may seem to be controlling financing companies, but it can also lead to similar past frauds in a system where the government is looking out for its supporters instead of citizens. By guaranteeing corruption by law, it can cause more harm. Our priority, however, should be to protect the rights of the citizen and to prevent those sacrifices from happening. Congressmans, we've done a good job in the Commission process. We've heard all the companies involved in this business, non-interested financing companies. All non-interested financing models have been bugged, but we haven't had a chance to listen to a citizen come into these companies with a dream of a house and a car. After the law came up, the victims called and complained. Therefore, when making the law, we must protect the rights of the most affected citizen, not the corporations. While this draft of the law is not comprehensive, I declare that we will support it. I salute the General Assembly.
